# macOS action inventory

Generated from Core `[ServiceCategory]`, `[ServiceAction]`, and `[MacCapability]` metadata. Do not edit this table directly; update the source contract or generator metadata.

The companion [`macos-action-inventory.json`](generated/macos-action-inventory.json) is the machine-readable source generated by the same build step.

`Partial` means the selected implementation exists but remains gated on its recorded real-Excel acceptance step. `Blocked` means the reviewed Apple Events and Office.js surfaces cannot preserve the exact public contract; use the recorded alternative.

**Progress:** 52/391 enabled; 339 gated.

**Statuses:** Blocked: 328; Implemented: 52; Partial: 11.

**Planned tiers for gated actions:** MacLimitation: 324; Native: 13; OptionalNativeHelper: 2.

| `conditionalformat.add-rule` | Adds a conditional formatting rule to a range. Excel COM: Range.FormatConditions.Add / AddColorScale / AddDatabar / AddIconSetCondition / AddTop10 / AddAboveAverage / AddUniqueValues. Basic rules (cellValue, expression) use operatorType/formula1/formula2 plus the interior/font/border format parameters. Visual rules use their own dedicated parameters (all optional; only the ones matching ruleType are read): - colorScale: colorScaleMin/Mid/Max Type/Value/Color (supply a mid stop for a 3-color scale) - dataBar: dataBarColor, dataBarNegativeColor, dataBarDirection, dataBarShowValue, dataBarMin/Max Type/Value - iconSet: iconSetId, iconSetReverse, iconSetShowIconOnly, iconThreshold1..4 Type/Value - top10: rank, top10Percent, topBottom - aboveAverage: aboveBelow - timePeriod: datePeriod<br>sheetName: string; required; Sheet name (empty for active sheet)<br>rangeAddress: string; required; Range address (A1 notation or named range)<br>ruleType: string; required; Rule type: cellValue (or cell-value), expression, colorScale, dataBar, top10, iconSet, uniqueValues, blanksCondition, timePeriod, aboveAverage. Both camelCase and kebab-case accepted.<br>operatorType: string?; optional; Required for cellValue rules. XlFormatConditionOperator: equal, notEqual, greater, less, greaterEqual, lessEqual, between, notBetween<br>formula1: string?; optional; Required for cellValue and expression rules. First formula/value for condition<br>formula2: string?; optional; Required for between/notBetween cellValue rules. Second formula/value<br>interiorColor: string?; optional; default null; Fill color (#RRGGBB or color index)<br>interiorPattern: string?; optional; default null; Interior pattern (1=Solid, -4142=None, 9=Gray50, etc.)<br>fontColor: string?; optional; default null; Font color (#RRGGBB or color index)<br>fontBold: bool?; optional; default null; Bold font<br>fontItalic: bool?; optional; default null; Italic font<br>borderStyle: string?; optional; default null; Border style: none, continuous, dash, dot, etc.<br>borderColor: string?; optional; default null; Border color (#RRGGBB or color index)<br>colorScaleMinType: string?; optional; default null; colorScale minimum stop type: minimum, number, percent, percentile, formula<br>colorScaleMinValue: string?; optional; default null; colorScale minimum stop value (for number/percent/percentile/formula)<br>colorScaleMinColor: string?; optional; default null; colorScale minimum stop color (#RRGGBB)<br>colorScaleMidType: string?; optional; default null; colorScale midpoint stop type (supply to create a 3-color scale)<br>colorScaleMidValue: string?; optional; default null; colorScale midpoint stop value<br>colorScaleMidColor: string?; optional; default null; colorScale midpoint stop color (#RRGGBB)<br>colorScaleMaxType: string?; optional; default null; colorScale maximum stop type: maximum, number, percent, percentile, formula<br>colorScaleMaxValue: string?; optional; default null; colorScale maximum stop value<br>colorScaleMaxColor: string?; optional; default null; colorScale maximum stop color (#RRGGBB)<br>dataBarColor: string?; optional; default null; dataBar fill color (#RRGGBB)<br>dataBarNegativeColor: string?; optional; default null; dataBar negative-value bar color (#RRGGBB)<br>dataBarDirection: string?; optional; default null; dataBar fill direction: context, leftToRight, rightToLeft<br>dataBarShowValue: bool?; optional; default null; dataBar show the cell value alongside the bar<br>dataBarMinType: string?; optional; default null; dataBar minimum point type: automaticMinimum, minimum, number, percent, percentile, formula<br>dataBarMinValue: string?; optional; default null; dataBar minimum point value<br>dataBarMaxType: string?; optional; default null; dataBar maximum point type: automaticMaximum, maximum, number, percent, percentile, formula<br>dataBarMaxValue: string?; optional; default null; dataBar maximum point value<br>iconSetId: string?; optional; default null; iconSet id: 3Arrows, 3TrafficLights1, 4Ratings, 5Quarters, etc.<br>iconSetReverse: bool?; optional; default null; iconSet reverse icon order<br>iconSetShowIconOnly: bool?; optional; default null; iconSet show only the icon (hide the value)<br>iconThreshold1Type: string?; optional; default null; iconSet threshold 1 type: percent, number, percentile, formula<br>iconThreshold1Value: string?; | `powerquery.refresh-all` | Refreshes every stored query that has a worksheet, Data Model, or workbook connection load. Parameter and connection-only staging queries have nothing to refresh on their own and are listed in skippedQueries; the loaded queries that use them re-evaluate them. Timeouts, cancellation, and Excel disconnects stop the whole operation. | `pythoninexcel.get-result` | Starts application-level calculation before polling, which can execute pending formulas in other workbooks owned by the Excel process. This is not an inspection-only action. Reads back the computed result of a =PY() cell, polling until the Python code (which executes asynchronously in Microsoft's cloud sandbox) finishes. Completion is detected deterministically from Excel's calculation state plus a per-cell #BUSY! guard, so a converged result is never confused with the in-flight #BUSY! placeholder. If the cloud backend is still busy at the deadline, the result reports failure and asks the caller to retry or raise maxWaitSeconds. A #NAME? result on a PY() formula is reported as Python in Excel being unavailable.<br>sheetName: string; required; Worksheet name<br>rangeAddress: string; required; Cell address containing the PY() formula<br>maxWaitSeconds: int; optional; default 30; Maximum seconds to poll for the cloud result before giving up (default: 30). Must be shorter than the session operation timeout. Returns as soon as the result is ready. | Native | Blocked; gated | Excel for Mac 16.113.2 accepted and preserved PY() Formula2 writes, but the result read failed through the public MCP entry point with Apple Events 'Message not understood'. Excel for Mac 16.113.2; Formula2 and Apple Events. | Python result values cannot be read reliably through the native API; use Windows for get-result. | Native | Dedicated Excel-authored pythoninexcel workbook with reversible literals and unrelated sentinel content. Add the action to a guarded Test-MacE2E capability slice and execute it through both excelcli and MCP. | Close only a confirmed session-owned workbook; preserve uncertain copies and never change unrecoverable shared Excel global state. CLI and MCP must prove exact validation, result shape, workbook effect, failure behavior, sentinel isolation, and save/reopen persistence when relevant. |

| `range.copy` | Copies a native rectangular range with an explicit paste kind. Formats/validation preserve content; formats also transfer number formats, protection, and applicable conditional rules. Formula paste includes constants and blanks. All kinds require unmerged rectangles and validated destination geometry, including transpose. Uses Excel's clipboard and clears the owned application's copy mode on exit.
